First run of ticket #32's tests, before any code for the ticket exists.
Branch ticket-32-answer-movement, on main at ecd04ef. Written by the test writer.

What ran, once each:
1. The new widget tests, app/test/answer_movement_test.dart, on the Mac (no device).
   Expected: the 5 movement tests fail because 4a and 4b don't move yet. The 4
   "nothing moves" tests for 4c, "too old", "couldn't load" and 4d pass, because
   nothing moves on those screens today and nothing should after the ticket.
2. The new integration tests, app/integration_test/answer_movement_driver.dart,
   on the Pixel_10 emulator (emulator-5554).
   Expected: all 7 fail because nothing moves. Each fails only at its movement
   check; the steps before it passed (the still 4a and 4b match ticket #31's
   screenshots and stay still for 10 seconds, and on the still screen the tap and
   back work and are timed).
3. The 3 updated tests of ticket #31 (answer_screens_driver.dart), on Pixel_10.
   Expected: pass. They only wait for the movement to end before measuring, and
   nothing moves yet.

Not counted as the first run: two earlier tries of the widget tests failed
because of the tests themselves, not the app. Their "time zero" was taken before
the new page was drawn on screen (Flutter lays a new page out off screen for its
first frame), so the still screens seemed to move after 48 ms. Fixed in
test/helpers/screen_watch.dart, along with two faults found while checking the
fix: the debug banner counted as part of the smiley, and the areas around the
words were too large for the confetti check.
